Existing benchmarks do not capture this class of problem, and those that attempt to evaluate open-ended reasoning rely on model-judged outputs that introduce noise and circularity. We present Hedge-Bench 1.0: a benchmark of 102 actual, on-the-job tasks grounded in the explicit reasoning traces of professional hedge fund analysts working with relevant information sources. This approach enables deterministic grading against verified expert steps. Frontier models and agents score below 16\% on the benchmark. We publish the dataset and evaluation harness at github.com/Trata-Inc/trata-hedge-bench.

</details>


### [294] [Entropy Is Not Enough: Unlocking Effective Reinforcement Learning for Visual Reasoning via Vision-Anchored Token Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03937)
*Senjie Jin,Peixin Wang,Boyang Liu,Xiaoran Fan,Shuo Li,Zhiheng Xi,Jiazheng Zhang,Yuhao Zhou,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态视觉推理中纯熵(token熵)作为信用分配机制会失效，并提出VEPO框架，通过视觉敏感性与token熵的乘法耦合来联合引导梯度信用分配，在视觉推理任务上显著优于纯熵基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文本RLVR中token熵被广泛认为是有效的信用分配机制，但该机制在视觉推理中是否仍然成立尚不明确。现有多模态RL方法要么缺乏系统性视觉度量，要么忽视了token熵主要驱动语义探索的事实，难以满足精确感知与语义推理交织的内在需求。

Method: 提出VEPO框架，通过将视觉敏感性与token熵进行原则性的乘法耦合，将梯度信用重新导向那些同时具有视觉基础和高信息量的token，从而在策略优化中实现视觉感知与语义推理的有效结合。

Result: VEPO在7B规模上超越纯熵基线2.28分，在3B规模上超越3.15分，消融实验进一步验证了方法的合理性。

Conclusion: 视觉推理中纯熵机制会因忽略低熵视觉敏感token而发生崩溃，VEPO的视觉-熵联合耦合方案有效解决了这一问题，为多模态RL中的信用分配提供了更优方案。

Abstract: While token-level entropy is commonly recognized as effective for credit assignment in text-only reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), it remains unclear whether this mechanism still holds in visual reasoning. Our controlled study shows that this mechanism collapses in visual reasoning due to the omission of vision-sensitive tokens with naturally low entropy. Although existing multimodal RL methods increasingly acknowledge the importance of visual perception, they struggle to satisfy the inherent demand for interleaving precise perceptual grounding with semantic reasoning, either lacking systematic visual measurements or overlooking that token entropy primarily drives semantic exploration. To address this, we introduce VEPO (Vision-Entropy token-selection for Policy Optimization), an effective RL framework explicitly integrating visual sensitivity with token entropy via a principled multiplicative coupling, where VEPO redirects gradient credit toward tokens which are simultaneously visually grounded and highly informative. Extensive experiments demonstrate VEPO's leading performance, significantly outperforming the entropy-only baseline by 2.28 points at 7B-scale and 3.15 points at 3B-scale. Ablations further substantiate the soundness of our method.

</details>


### [295] [Imaginative Perception Tokens Enhance Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03988)
*Mahtab Bigverdi,Lindsey Li,Weikai Huang,Yiming Liu,Jaemin Cho,Jieyu Zhang,Tuhin Kundu,Chris Dangjoo Kim,Zelun Luo,Linda Shapiro,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出想象感知令牌(IPT)，一种中间感知表征，用于增强VLM在不可直接观察场景下的空间推理能力。在三个新任务(视角推理、路径追踪、多视角计数)上，IPT监督持续优于文本思维链训练，且在多视角计数任务上提升3.4%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在空间推理中表现不佳，尤其是当关键信息无法直接观察时（如从不可见视角推理、穿过遮挡区域追踪路径、整合部分观察），而文本思维链存在模态不匹配问题。

Method: 提出IPT作为中间感知表征，将VLM在替代空间配置下的感知结果外部化。基于统一VLM BAGEL骨干网络，在三个任务（PET、PT、MVC）约2万个样本上进行IPT监督训练。

Result: IPT监督持续提升空间推理性能，在MVC上提升3.4%准确率，在PT上达到与强闭源模型竞争的性能；IPT与标签监督结合可进一步获益，而文本思维链可能大幅降低性能。

Conclusion: IPT为推理不可观察空间结构提供了原理性的监督信号，在提升泛化能力的同时生成可解释的中间表征，且避免了语言模态强制处理空间计算带来的不匹配问题。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) excel at many tasks but still struggle with spatial reasoning when critical information is not directly observable. Many such problems require imaginative perception: inferring what would be seen from an unseen viewpoint, tracing paths through occluded spaces, or integrating partial observations into a coherent spatial representation. We introduce Imaginative Perception Tokens (IPT), intermediate perceptual representations that externalize what a VLM would perceive under alternative spatial configurations while remaining consistent with the observed input.
  To study this capability, we formulate three tasks, Perspective Taking (PET), Path Tracing (PT), and Multiview Counting (MVC), and construct datasets of approximately 20K examples with ground truth imaginations, answers, and evaluation benchmarks. Using the unified VLM BAGEL as the backbone, IPT supervision consistently improves spatial reasoning and often outperforms textual chain of thought training, even without generating images at inference time. On MVC, IPT improves accuracy by 3.4% and achieves competitive performance with strong closed-source models on PT. We further find that combining IPT and label-only supervision yields additional gains, whereas textual chain of thought can substantially degrade performance, suggesting a modality mismatch when spatial computation is forced through language. Overall, IPT provides a principled supervision signal for reasoning about unobserved spatial structure, improving generalization while producing interpretable intermediate representations.

</details>


### [296] [VESTA: Visual Exploration with Statistical Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00384)
*William Rudman,Abhishek Divekar,Kanishk Jain,Sebastian Joseph,Stella S. R. Offner,Matthew Lease,Kyle Mahowald,Greg Durrett,Junyi Jessy Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VESTA是一个框架，通过让视觉-语言模型动态创建和积累诊断工具（数据变换、可视化、统计检验），在模型拟合任务中显著优于现有方法，并引入了DAWN基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将定量模型拟合到数据是科学工作流的核心步骤，但自动化程度最低。现有基于VLM的智能体系统在复杂建模任务上表现不佳。

Method: 提出VESTA框架，赋予VLM动态增长的探索工具箱，通过数据变换、假设驱动可视化和稳健统计检验指导模型精炼。工具在上下文中积累并可复用。包含三种配置：无工具、静态专家编写工具、动态模型编写工具。引入DAWN基准数据集（分布拟合和时间序列建模，含天文学任务）。

Result: VESTA的动态工具创建优于先前智能体流水线，在复杂和领域特定任务上提升最大。动态生成工具比现有视觉工具创建系统更复杂，每个函数覆盖更多诊断类别，且强烈偏好VLM评论者可直接推理的可视化输出。

Conclusion: 动态工具创建和积累机制有效提升了VLM在科学建模中的能力，尤其适用于复杂和领域特定的任务场景。

Abstract: Fitting quantitative models to data is a central step in scientific workflows, yet it remains one of the least automated. Recent agent-based systems leverage language and vision-language models (VLMs) to iteratively propose and refine statistical models, but these systems struggle on more challenging modeling tasks. To address these limitations, we introduce VESTA: Visual Exploration with Statistical Tool Agents, a framework that equips VLMs with a dynamically growing exploration toolkit to guide model refinement through data transformations, hypothesis-driven visualizations, and robust statistical tests. Unlike prior systems that rely on iterative critique alone, VESTA actively explores data before and during refinement by selecting or creating diagnostic tools, which accumulate in the model's context and can be reused later. We evaluate VESTA against established baselines in three toolkit configurations: no tools, static expert-written tools, and dynamic model-written tools. To support this evaluation, we introduce DAWN (Dataset for Automated Workflows and Numerical Modeling), a benchmark targeting distribution fitting and time series modeling with varying difficulty tiers, and culminating in real-world astronomy tasks including modeling initial mass functions and gravitational-wave chirp signals. We find that VESTA's dynamic tool creation outperforms prior agentic pipelines, with the largest gains on complex and domain-specific tasks. We further show that dynamically generated tools are substantially more sophisticated than those produced by existing visual tool-creation systems, covering more diagnostic categories per function and strongly preferring visual outputs that the VLM critic can reason over directly.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [297] [Fewer, Better Frames: A Compute-Normalized Proof of Concept for Coherence-First World-Model Rendering with Model-Guided FSR4 Frame Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02586)
*Paweł Katarzyński*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"一致性优先"的世界模型推理策略，在15 FPS下生成高上下文锚帧，通过潜在运动引导和合成深度重建至30 FPS。相比原生的30 FPS基线方法，该方法在路径几何、物体身份、轮廓和深度层次保持方面表现更优，LPIPS指标在所有测试场景中均占优。实验在森林、剑、沙漠和雪地场景中进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统世界模型评估采用原生帧率，但更高标称帧率会牺牲长程场景稳定性。本文旨在探索在有限推理预算下，通过"一致性优先"分配策略（而非帧率优先）来平衡帧率与场景稳定性的可行性。

Method: 基于Overworld的Waypoint-1.5系列和WorldEngine运行时，在Windows回退栈上使用ONNX Runtime + DirectML及FSR4 DX12桥接实现。一致性优先分支以15 FPS生成高上下文锚帧，利用潜在增量运动引导和合成深度重建至30 FPS；与原生30 FPS的低上下文基线方法在相同种子、路线、控制脚本、目标呈现时长和局部时间缩放条件下进行比较。

Result: 一致性优先分支在路径几何、物体身份、大轮廓和深度层次保持方面优于基线方法；基线方法更早出现亮度漂移和几何失真。LPIPS指标在所有场景中均偏好一致性优先分支。计算成本大致匹配（相同GPU、相同时间尺度）。单独的剑场景探测表明存在局部非单调性：更多上下文和去噪并不自动提升质量。

Conclusion: 一致性优先分配作为有限推理预算下的概念验证策略是可行的，但尚未成为可用的实时渲染器。结果支持在推理预算受限时优先保证场景一致性而非单纯追求高帧率的思路。

Abstract: World models are often evaluated by native frame cadence, but higher nominal frame rate can trade away long-horizon scene stability. This article reports an independent proof of concept implemented using Overworld's Waypoint-1.5 family and WorldEngine runtime on a Windows fallback stack with ONNX Runtime + DirectML and an FSR4 DX12 bridge. The tested coherence-first branch generates higher-context anchor frames at a 15 FPS presentation-timeline cadence and reconstructs presentation to 30 FPS using latent-delta motion guidance and synthesized depth. It is compared against a lower-context cadence-first baseline that generates about 30 FPS natively under the same seed, route, control script, target presentation duration, and local time-scaling regime. Across forest, sword, desert, and snow scenes, the coherence-first branch preserves path geometry, object identity, large silhouettes, and depth layering longer, while the baseline degrades earlier into brightness drift and geometric distortion. Lightweight temporal metrics and paired videos support the visual comparison, with LPIPS favoring the coherence-first branch across all tested scenes. Here compute-normalized means approximately matched same-GPU, same-timescale operating points, not exact FLOP parity or measured realtime throughput. A separate heavier sword-scene probe suggests local non-monotonicity: more context and denoising did not automatically improve quality. These results support coherence-first allocation as a practical proof-of-concept strategy under limited inference budget, not as a finished realtime renderer.

</details>


### [298] [A Novel Procedural Generation for Level Design of Mansions and Dungeons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03857)
*Isaac Fiuza Vieira,Kathya Silvia Collazos Linares,Esteban Walter Gonzalez Clua,Érick Oliveira Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于关卡设计原则的程序化内容生成方法，通过BSP空间分割、图遍历连接房间和后处理三个阶段，生成结构连贯且可导航的室内环境。实验表明，在适当参数下超过91%的生成地图实现了完全连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCG方法缺乏与关卡设计原则的对齐，容易产生空间结构不连贯和较差的游戏体验。本文旨在设计一种融合关卡设计原则的PCG方法，以保证生成室内环境的建筑连贯性和可导航性。

Method: 方法分为三个阶段：(1) 使用二叉空间分割(BSP)对空间进行分割；(2) 基于图遍历对房间进行逻辑连接，避免冗余链接；(3) 后处理阶段清理结构伪影并提升视觉连贯性。方法支持房间面积和形状的参数化，并通过种子控制随机性以实现可复现性。

Result: 进行了两项实验：第一项展示了方法在不同种子和参数配置下的灵活性；第二项使用BFS验证了地图的可导航性，在生成的100,000张地图中，超过91%实现了完全连通性。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效生成建筑结构连贯、可导航性强的室内环境，在保证生成质量的同时提供了参数化灵活性和随机可复现性。

Abstract: Procedural Content Generation (PCG) has become an essential technique in game development due to its ability to reduce production time and cost while increasing replayability and variety. However, when not aligned with level design principles, PCG can lead to incoherent spatial structures and poor gameplay experiences. Objective: This work proposes a PCG method guided by level design principles to generate structured indoor environments - such as houses, mansions, and dungeons - aiming to ensure both architectural coherence and navigability. Methodology: The method is divided into three main stages: segmentation of the space using Binary Space Partitioning (BSP); logical connection of rooms based on graph traversal to prevent redundant links; and a post-processing stage responsible for cleaning structural artifacts and improving visual cohesion. The methodology allows parameterization of room area and shape, with randomness controlled via seeds for reproducibility. Results: Two experiments were conducted. The first demonstrated the flexibility of the methodology under different seeds and parameter configurations. The second evaluated the navigability of generated maps by verifying connectivity using Breadth-First Search (BFS). In this test, 100,000 maps were generated, and with suitable parameters, over 91% of them achieved complete connectivity.

</details>


### [299] [PaintBench: Deterministic Evaluation of Precise Visual Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00188)
*Kai Xu,Ellis Brown,Shrikar Madhu,Rob Fergus,He He,Saining Xie*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: PaintBench是一个针对精确视觉编辑任务的动态可扩展基准测试，涵盖四大类20种基本操作。对11个图像编辑模型的评估显示性能普遍较低（最高17.1% mIoU），且任务分解揭示了各操作类型的难度差异。通过与数据可视化编辑任务（TinyGrafixBench）的强相关性（R²=0.91）验证了其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型在开放式视觉编辑方面表现良好，但执行精确的单答案编辑仍然是一个重要障碍。现有评估方法缺乏精确、可控且抗污染的基准，因此需要构建一个能够严格衡量和推动精确视觉编辑进步的评估框架。

Method: 1) 设计PaintBench基准，包含20种基础精确视觉编辑操作，分为几何变换、结构操纵、颜色变化和符号推理四类。2) 采用程序化生成方式，通过可配置复杂度实现无限且抗污染的评估套件。3) 使用确定性像素级评估（mIoU）替代有偏见的裁判模型。4) 创建TinyGrafixBench作为数据可视化编辑任务，验证PaintBench得分的泛化能力。

Result: 1) 11个图像编辑模型中最高分仅17.1%（mIoU），整体性能低下。2) 任务分解发现几何变换、大部分结构操纵和基于公式的颜色变化尤为困难，且存在模型特异性专长。3) 场景变化（目标数量、背景复杂度、颜色方案、编辑区域大小）会导致性能下降。4) PaintBench与TinyGrafixBench得分呈强线性相关（R²=0.91，p<0.001）。

Conclusion: PaintBench为衡量和推动精确多模态视觉编辑的进步提供了严格的评估基础。当前模型在该任务上表现普遍不足，需要进一步研究和发展。

Abstract: While current multimodal models are proficient at open-ended visual editing, executing precise single-answer edits remains an important obstacle. To probe this challenge, we introduce PaintBench, a dynamically scalable benchmark targeting 20 fundamental precise visual editing operations across four categories: geometric transformation, structural manipulation, color change, and symbolic reasoning. Procedural generation with configurable complexity enables an effectively infinite, contamination-resistant evaluation suite, and deterministic pixel-level evaluation eliminates reliance on bias-prone judge models. Across 11 image editing models, we find overall low performance, with the current highest-performing industry leader scoring only 17.1% (mIoU). Task decomposition reveals especially challenging operation types (geometric transformation, most structural manipulation, formula-based color change) and model-specific specializations. Fine-grained benchmark diagnostics further show performance degradations induced by scene variations in object count, background complexity, color scheme, and edit-region size. To test generalization of PaintBench scores to applied task performance, we create a procedural, deterministic evaluation for data visualization editing (TinyGrafixBench) and find strong linear correlation with PaintBench scores ($R^2 = 0.91$, $p < 0.001$). Altogether, PaintBench provides a rigorous foundation for measuring and driving progress in precise multimodal visual editing.

</details>
